## Fast iteration and Apple-like liquid controls · 2026-11-13 (FAST-ITERATE-29 / LIQUID-APPLE-30)

Latest user direction: “以及我们现在的液态玻璃效果我也希望能够做到苹果那种效果”; then “你现在进度有点慢了啊子代理拉起来，别review代码了快速开发快速提交快速cicd我来验证反馈”. This explicitly stops additional review as a delivery gate. Implement independent areas in parallel, submit each concrete finished batch directly to latest main and confirm CI triggers, then let the user validate the real artifact. The existing ban on tests/local builds/probes remains. Earlier review notes below describe work already performed, not a renewed gate.

LIQUID-APPLE-30 targets the existing interactive liquid roles' clear background transport, edge refraction/highlight, local pressure and nearby merging through the official Haze 2.1 pipeline. Apple system appearance is a quality target, not a claim of identical rendering on every platform. Preserve PANEL/POPOVER/DRAWER frosting, the settings cards and the new card-free detail information. Do not add a content-wide glass wrapper, a new renderer/dependency or permanent decorative animation; retain platform/accessibility fallbacks and truthful device-evidence boundaries. Implemented in LiquidGlass.kt: lower milky tint/white point, stronger official edge refraction and directional specular, bounded local pressure light/refraction, and retained nearby container merging. Gallery keeps its graduated diffusion/mask; PANEL/POPOVER/DRAWER and accessibility/platform fallbacks remain. These are engineering adaptations, not Apple-published parameter values or proven pixel equivalence. Sources read: `https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/materials.md` and `https://chrisbanes.github.io/haze/2.1.0/effects/glass/` / `https://chrisbanes.github.io/haze/2.1.0/containers/`. No further review gate, tests, local build or probes; actual material/legibility/feel awaits user artifact feedback.

| ID | Implemented boundary and observable requirement |
| --- | --- |
| SOURCE-PERF-14A | The six registered foreground search sources start independently rather than waiting through three two-source waves. Preserve each adapter's concurrency/rate limits, real Retry-After, cancellation and 15s source deadline, plus the separate two-slot background catalogue limit. Ordinary requests/pages reuse the existing per-source cache; only explicit refresh/retry forces it. A force caller waiting on the existing lock may reuse an identical successful response completed after its own start, never an older pre-click result. No detached search jobs or new runtime. |
| SOURCE-PERF-14B | Cache successful source pages for the existing five-minute interval, empty successes for 60s, failures never; bound the existing cache to 64 pages and 1200 actual hits. Origin invalidation clears selected search terms and discovery TTL, with an epoch preventing a late old scan from repopulating them. Query-local bounded derived caches reuse normalized titles/authors/relevance; status-only callbacks retain existing groups, and mutable derived work stays outside StateFlow CAS retries. No global mutable ICU instance. |
| SOURCE-PERF-14C | Combine the two revision signals and collectLatest. Build unique visible work projections once on Default, checking current request/work/reader epochs and both revisions before application. Keep the actual current source and page; save only changed detail catalogues with serialized current-result guards. Move the network-detail path's final semantic merge to Default too. Existing direct cached-open paths and per-source striped lock collisions remain bounded follow-ups, not claimed solved. No measured latency/FPS percentage is inferred from these source changes. |
| SOURCE-LIBRARY-14 | Settings → 漫画源 manages the registered built-in source library: enabled/disabled, reversible removal and re-addition of installed adapters. Copy is protected from removal because existing account/tag/community capability depends on it; it may still be excluded from global title search/automatic matching. This protection is an implementation default, not a claimed user-supplied list of core IDs. Switches stop global search and automatic discovery/linked refresh; explicit reading/manual source access and existing records/downloads are preserved. Removed adapters remain compiled in the APK and are recoverable from the add list. Persist IO success before publishing state, validate installed IDs and never accept an externally stored flag that removes core protection. Search cache v7 includes the enabled-set scope and configuration changes clear/restart stale results, cancel old automatic work and reject late publication. Zero enabled sources produces an explicit settings action message, not a fabricated empty match. |
| READER-SOURCE-14 | The pictured lower reader row becomes one deliberate source chooser target: X offsets only, current actual chapter source drawn at the front, rear marks partially occluded, no Y drift/rotation. Clicking the cluster opens the existing anchored/frosted selector and never immediately changes source. Only choosing a real row starts the existing preparation and optional same-page prompt. Use a meaningful accessible hit region without moving chapter grid geometry or adding a management submenu. |
| SOURCE-MARKS-14 | All body-source marks use shared local crisp vectors designed for MangaFlow. Keep the muted-blue Copy wordmark; use distinct, consistently scaled symbols for the other installed sources and real source names in semantics/selection. Remove official favicon requests/failure tracking, pixel scaling and text-name-as-tiny-logo fallbacks. These are app source markers, not claimed official brand assets. No raster/image/model dependency is introduced. |

Extension capability remains explicit: this batch manages installed built-in adapters; it does not implement arbitrary website, Legado JSON, Mihon APK or JavaScript extension import. Mihon's official documentation describes external extension installation/uninstallation (`https://mihon.app/docs/faq/browse/extensions`), and its loader is an Android APK/host API boundary. Mangayomi's published JS development contract requires search/detail/page-list methods and host HTTP/DOM bridges, beyond a base URL (`https://github.com/kodjodevf/mangayomi-extensions/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING-JS.md`; that extension repository is archived). A future cross-platform extension engine should be evaluated separately from Android-only APK hosting; no source-import button may falsely claim that this runtime exists. ## Reader flow, source pressure and offline versions · 2026-11-13 (READING-FLOW-26; device/service acceptance pending)

Current user request: “增加一个下滑回上一话的功能以及我问个问题现在缓存压力加载速度压力是不是更大了这么多源，以及我需要把那个智能分镜功能关闭隐藏掉先”; “现在标签搜索一直出不了结果我怀疑和你的多源有关以及现在多源了那个下载是不是也得改改”, followed by “继续” and “？”. Exact message IDs are unavailable. Preserve the concurrent `89bdaf3` federation/source-row/source-resume changes and `9eb59ca` shared foundation.

- **26A / previous chapter:** in vertical reading, a new downward pull starting at the real chapter beginning uses the existing AndroidX-derived edge physics in reverse: 96dp physical threshold, resistance, hysteresis, release/cancel semantics, reduced motion and one semantic threshold event. The first image must be loaded and its natural height committed; restoration, ordinary downward scrolling from interior pages, flings and cancelled/multipointer gestures cannot arm it. Previous/next share one chapter-change guard, stay within the current chapter group and retain real per-chapter saved positions. Reuse the compact chapter footer at the top only while pulling; no new permanent reader panel, zoom or decorative paging haptics. Horizontal paging keeps its existing controls/gestures.
- **26B / temporarily pause panels:** supersede PANEL-01–08's user-facing availability. `PanelReadingAvailable=false` masks even previously enabled per-book/global settings to OFF, hides the settings section and returns from model UI before stores/runtimes initialize. Existing model files, preferences and panel code remain for a later explicit re-enable; ordinary reading does not enter the guided renderer. This is a complete product pause, not merely removing its switch while an old engine remains active.
- **26C / search:** TAG remains a real Copy-category query, distinct from the new federated title search. The reported blanket service failure is not reproduced here. Confirmed gaps are typed TAG text being only a chip filter even on Search, exact-only simplified/traditional matching, and unresolved foreign labels returning no Copy index. Search now commits a recognized tag to the selected real paths, normalized names/common equivalent labels share the actual catalog identifiers, and chip suggestions include that mapping. Only Copy-origin metadata contributes native paths; foreign paths never masquerade as Copy filters. Unknown labels show a selection/error state instead of inventing title results. Tag-index lookup has the shared foreground deadline. Multi-tag intersection, native pagination and source errors stay truthful.
- **26D / pressure:** more sources add metadata/search requests and catalog memory. Existing auto-discovery/background catalog concurrency is two, each source has a 25s budget, retained raw catalogs are capped at96, and image manifests already have a bounded 32-entry/60s cache. Automatic source discovery does not fetch every source's body images. Add 64 bounded source/query/page search results with a five-minute TTL and 60s fresh-directory reuse; fixed 16-way locks coalesce concurrent identical preparation. Domain generations prevent reusing invalidated data, raw replacements clear freshness stamps, and explicit force refresh bypasses TTL. Foreground title search relies on BrowseCache for reuse and bypasses this repository TTL so refresh/retry still reaches the source. These are source-observed limits, not measured RAM/bandwidth/frame-rate improvements; first-time federation still makes additional requests.
- **26E / downloads:** retain qualified chapter storage IDs, canonical work IDs, existing manifest bytes/headers, hashes, queues, pause/retry/delete and local files. Separate logical “some version is offline” presentation from exact selected-ID download eligibility, so downloading A no longer blocks B. Source choice rows can explicitly queue a validated variant and the download list names its source. A foreign canonical work can contain a native Copy chapter ID, so network preparation, reading, prefetch, retries, covers and saved-picture headers use explicit Chapter source ownership via the shared `chapterSourceMangaId`; they never change history/download keys. Preparation uses the foreground timeout and captures the selected work before enqueueing. No automatic bulk downloading of all versions.

Validation is source/diff/whitespace inspection and independent read-only review only. No tests, local build, API probes or device operation; submit to latest main and confirm the existing CI trigger, preserving manual-only Lint, all signing/R8/startup protections and the current shared migration. Actual pull feel, backend TAG results, download/resume correctness and resource use require the replacement artifact and real user operation; source existence is not acceptance.

## First-tap drawer selection · 2026-11-13 (DRAWER-TAP-24; device confirmation pending)

User: “侧边栏有时候得点两下才能切换什么情况？”. At main `34280ab`, the public BOM 2026.09.00 POM resolves Material3 1.4.0 and Foundation/UI 1.12.1. Their actual published source JARs were read: NavigationDrawer.kt appends anchoredDraggable after the caller modifier; AnchoredDraggable.kt defaults immediate capture to isAnimationRunning; Draggable.kt consumes the initial DOWN when no slop wait is needed. This provides a concrete path where the first tap catches drawer travel and Surface never sees a valid click. The official API documents that animation capture default: `https://developer.android.com/reference/kotlin/androidx/compose/foundation/gestures/anchoredDraggable.modifier`. This is source evidence, not a captured phone reproduction; the installed version is not specified.

Keep native drawer physics, DRAWER-RESPONSE-22's route/reveal and the latest responsive layout. Put one local tap observer in the caller modifier, before the native draggable. On DOWN only, enable it for drawer animation when the vertical menu is not scrolling; settled gestures stay on the existing Surface. Hit-test actual measured row bounds and capture the target and original root rectangle. Compare finger movement in root coordinates so drawer travel is not mistaken for a swipe. Never consume DOWN/MOVE. Reject real slop, multipointer, out-of-bounds and cancellation; accept only a real unconsumed Touch/Stylus UP, consume that UP to prevent a duplicate Surface click, and use the same guarded selection callback as mouse/keyboard/TalkBack. The observer precedes native Initial-pass consumption, so consumed synthetic CANCEL cannot turn into a selection. Do not recheck animation at UP: a legitimate catch has already stopped it. No delayed gesture replay, selection-on-press, disabled side swipes, internal API or second drawer implementation.

No tests, local build or device operation; submit to current main and confirm its normal CI trigger. User: “要不先把现在的代码的多种设备屏幕适配做好再做宽平台？还是说不同平台的多设备适配其实都得再写一套”, then “那交给你去做了关于这个去吧彻底完成”, “直接在 main 处理这个屏幕适配哈” and “继续”. Exact message IDs are unavailable. Scope: improve the current Android app in original main, organize reusable layout policy, retain existing functionality/design; KMP/iOS/desktop builds and the separate repository are outside this slice.

- **01 / viewport:** `ui/AdaptiveLayout.kt` exposes the measured Compose window and a small immutable geometry policy using Compose/Kotlin only. Geometry no longer uses Android screenWidthDp/screenHeightDp; locale reads remain platform-specific. The adaptation batch originally had no shared module; KMP-FOUNDATION now moves this same policy into `shared/src/commonMain`, consumed by the existing Android app.
- **02 / density:** discovery/latest retain their saved list or 2–4-column setting as a maximum, reducing columns for narrow windows/enlarged type. The shelf uses up to three/four/six columns by available width. Chapter/source grids can reduce to one column. Resizing does not overwrite preferences, chapter order, source IDs or history.
- **03 / wide content:** wallpaper and capture remain full-window; browse/shelf, detail, lists, account and settings have separate centered maximum widths. Vertical original-image pages have an 864dp width cap and keep their aspect ratio/full-resolution tile path; horizontal spreads retain their center-spine alignment. Recommendation art becomes shorter in short windows.
- **04 / chrome:** the existing segmented discovery header moves browse controls to a second measured row only when necessary, retaining the upper-right search anchor and real rank-tab origin. Shelf/download selectors reserve the constrained width of their single permanent morph surface; tall menus scroll above the bottom safe area. Drawer navigation scrolls independently from its heading/footer. Keep current Haze roles and Feel ownership.
- **05 / continuity:** roots respect safe top/horizontal insets; reader controls respect visibility-independent system bars, cutouts and caption clearance. Orientation/screen-size/layout changes preserve the activity/reader route. Vertical scrolling scales its in-memory pixel offset by the changed image viewport width, preserving position without a new storage format or synthetic reading event.
- **06 / panels:** dialogs measure their own safe/IME-constrained viewport and pass it to their scrollable contents. Reader directories use remaining height rather than a forced minimum. Sheets are width/height bounded. Account supporting text/errors/token import scroll separately, leaving room for the unchanged official login WebView. Existing focus/dismissal, signed login ownership and retained exit motion stay intact.
- **07 / enlarged text:** font scale can change actual columns, selector height, comment-source choices and statistics-header arrangement. Settings title/value share remaining space; bottom settings content clears navigation/IME. Keep saved list positions, current chapter, source ownership, real counts and download intent.
- **08 / evidence:** source/diff review and direct fresh-main integration, preserving concurrent work, followed by confirmation that existing main CI triggered. No local build, Lint run, executable tests, API probes, emulator or UI automation. User acceptance covers discovery/search/detail/reader/shelf/downloads/settings/account at compact/wide widths, short landscape/split-screen and enlarged fonts; resize with an open reader, selector or keyboard. Actual visual proportions, gestures, position continuity, hinge/fold placement and performance remain pending user feedback. Source review is not runtime acceptance.

Basis read in this thread: Kotlin Multiplatform “Adaptive layouts”, Android “Use window size classes” and Kotlin Multiplatform “Default UI behavior on different platforms”. Use the current Foundation primitives and existing attributed UI; no new dependency, model or runtime bundle is added.

## Drawer destination handoff · 2026-11-13 (DRAWER-CONTINUITY-17; source correction, device acceptance pending)

User: “现在点侧边栏切换会很卡卡一下才切换”. The current source awaited native drawer.close() before changeSection, so destination composition began only after the close had finished. This is a confirmed serial delay in the implementation, not a frame-time trace or proof that it is the only device bottleneck. This follow-up replaces DRAWER-CONTINUITY-16's close-first destination rule.

- Keep one reference-keyed DrawerAction, immediate menu selection feedback, the native DrawerState/frosted sheet, actual-offset background relationship, saved root positions and existing input gates.
- For a different root destination, commit sectionName and the drawer root-transition marker while the drawer is stationary. The destination's active AnimatedContent entry acknowledges its committed composition after the next frame. Only then begin native close. The close therefore reveals the prepared destination rather than exposing the old page and then starting the new page. Do not wait for network/data completion, preload all roots, add a fixed delay, or start a second full-page entrance.
- Same-section taps close immediately. Reduced-motion requests snap. If detail/reader state takes over while waiting, stop waiting for a root that is no longer active; do not replay a late destination after close.
- Only the matching active root can acknowledge a selection. New actions and normal/deep-link navigation still invalidate old work by identity. Selection commits before drawer exit, so cancelling a later close does not roll back a legitimate already-committed root. Cancellation must not replay an old destination or clear a newer request.
- Independent read-only review found an existing restart race when motion preference changes: the cancelled actor's finally could clear the same request before its replacement starts. Cleanup now requires both matching identity and an active coroutine; the new actor can retain that same request, while native mutation cancellation and real-user gesture behavior remain subject to device acceptance.
- Keep the existing Haze source node stable and all manual scrim/gesture/input ownership. Destination composition is separated from the moving-close phase through an actual composition/frame acknowledgement, rather than moving both jobs into the same click.

Scope: MangaFlowApp routing/frame acknowledgement only, plus these existing entry documents. No alternate navigation framework, universal root-page cache, SDK change or data migration. Preserve current optional native-runtime downloads, dock/filter improvements, ranking/tab/search/heatmap work, Haze 2.1, auth/startup, signatures and manual-only full Lint. Source/diff/whitespace and independent read-only review; no local build, tests, emulator or UI/API probes. Confirm latest-main CI trigger only. Actual tap latency, first-frame cost, interruption and phone smoothness remain pending user feedback.

## Optional inference components · 2026-11-13 (OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-01–06; source implemented, CI/device acceptance pending)

User: “？这个运行库一定要加吗”, then “都弄成可选下载吧现在太大了”. These follow the verified 0.1.131 package-size finding in APK-SIZE-01 and authorize moving both native inference engines out of the base APK, in addition to the already optional model weights. Preserve all detection choices and traditional offline reading. This replaces the bundled-engine/compression-only decision, rather than removing high-precision or learned-order features.

| ID | Implemented behavior and observable requirement |
| --- | --- |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-01 | The main APK retains small LiteRT/ONNX Java APIs and MangaFlow's compiled adapters, but excludes libtensorflowlite_jni.so, libonnxruntime.so and libonnxruntime4j_jni.so for every ABI. Native binaries are not repackaged under assets or renamed. Existing model weights remain user-requested downloads. Traditional recognition does not download or initialize a native engine. CI inspects the actual APK ZIP and stops publication if these libraries or model weights are still bundled. No exact replacement APK size is claimed before its actual artifact exists. |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-02 | Reader management separates “运行组件” from “模型”. LiteRT serves Nano; one ONNX component serves both Koharu and Inkwell. Download only the running app process's compatible ABI, expose progress/cancel/retry/delete and preserve previous model files. Selecting an unready AI mode gives honest traditional/geometry fallback; component installation updates the current reader's readiness effect. Closing management leaves an explicit download running. Nothing is silently downloaded merely by opening settings or selecting a mode. |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-03 | CI stages the exact pinned LiteRT 1.4.2 and ONNX 1.31.1 AARs, preserving original library bytes and licenses. It emits two engines × four ABIs as deterministic ZIP assets. runtime-catalog.json is a SHA256withRSA envelope signed with the installed APK's same permanent key and contains app/release identity, engine version, ABI, canonical release URL, archive size/hash and each library's name/size/hash. All component assets, catalog, APK and checksums publish together under existing draft/stale-main/tag/immutability guards. Ordinary build artifacts contain unsigned components and are not represented as installable official runtime catalogs. |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-04 | The downloader obtains the catalog only from the current app version's release, verifies its RSA signature against the installed certificate, checks app identity/version, picks the process ABI and validates archive/library SHA256 plus ELF architecture. Only exact declared native names are written as ordinary files into a fresh noBackup private staging directory; ZIP mode/link attributes are not inherited and license members are not extracted. Bounded sizes, HTTPS/closed redirect hosts, duplicate/path checks, synced writes, read-only sealing and atomic directory installation remain mandatory. A signed local receipt and every installed library are reverified on startup, without networking; identical compatible SDK versions/ABIs can be reused after an app update. No public writable/external native path or unverified fallback is allowed. |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-05 | LiteRT 1.4.2 supports same-App-ClassLoader absolute System.load before TensorFlowLite.init; the exact AAR Java initialization contract was statically inspected. ORT 1.31.1's Android branch ignores the desktop native.path/skip properties, so adapt its actual MIT OnnxRuntime.java Android loading branch to the verified immutable absolute directory, core before JNI. Filter the original loader classes from the build-generated API JAR to avoid duplicate types; preserve the rest of the pinned APIs/native signatures. The path is published before native loading. No downloaded DEX/JAR, reflection-based classloader mutation or plugin framework is introduced. Source identities and differences are in third_party/onnxruntime/ADAPTATION.md. |
| OPTIONAL-RUNTIME-06 | Loading is serialized and completes noncancellably once dlopen starts. Cancellation during checksum verification is rethrown, not reported as corruption. Native failures leave traditional reading available and require a fresh process before retry/removal. Android cannot unload these libraries within the same process: removal after a load/partial load records “重启后删除” in noBackup storage without unlinking live files, then deletes before any load during the next process initialization. Pending removal blocks replacement downloads. Existing image quality, original page files, panel cache/progress, Haze, account and reader controls remain. |

Sources actually read: pinned [ORT 1.31.1 OnnxRuntime.java](https://github.com/microsoft/onnxruntime/blob/v1.31.1/java/src/main/java/ai/onnxruntime/OnnxRuntime.java), exact official LiteRT 1.4.2 AAR/class initialization metadata and [upstream TensorFlowLite.java](https://github.com/google-ai-edge/LiteRT/blob/19c67cbdb91f1fe49d0edb4fc85e5da59ef03b29/tflite/java/src/main/java/org/tensorflow/lite/TensorFlowLite.java), Android API-26 native loader source, and Gradle's provider-backed file dependency documentation. The desktop ORT property documentation alone is insufficient for Android. Existing engine/model licenses remain separately identified, and component ZIPs include the applicable original runtime notices.

Scope: build-generated filtered API JAR, native exclusion/staging, optional runtime store, existing model/camera readiness and management, same-signer packaging/publication plus the bounded upstream loader adaptation. Independent source reviews cover the loader/build API correspondence and the catalog/ZIP/downloader contract. Python AST, workflow YAML, embedded shell syntax and source/diff integrity are inspected without running the application or packaging scripts locally. No local Gradle build, tests, device/API probes, native/model execution, synthetic screenshots or successful phone-loading claim. Submit directly on latest main; only the existing CI trigger is confirmed at delivery. Replacement APK size, actual runtime download/loading, SDK memory behavior and device experience remain pending their real artifact/user evidence.

Keep the filter as an interactive sibling. Closing resumes the original list and pagination eligibility; apply source/long-review/promotion filters immediately and preserve manual recovery. Do not cancel/replace authenticated or source requests, freeze fake responses, or clear list data.

Reuse is the existing navigation softSurfaceShadow, LiquidGlassSurface role policy, beUI-derived retained panel/input gates and finite motion roles. No new component dependency or alternative renderer. Source findings identify avoidable work and a long scale tail; they do not establish a profiled device jank root cause or frame-rate improvement. ## Faster releases and APK download size · 2026-11-13 (FAST-RELEASE-02 / APK-SIZE-01 / UPDATE-NETWORK-01)

User feedback: “现在怎么久？我滴妈呀 lint 没必要了吧其实” (1000121541.jpg), followed by “100 多 md？你直接把模型塞进去了吗” (1000121542.jpg). The release screenshot shows full Lint at 6m25s and assembly at 3m30s; the update screenshot reports 157.2 MB for 0.1.131. These are observed before states, not a timing or package-size guarantee for the correction.

- FAST-RELEASE-02 supersedes the mandatory-full-Lint-on-every-push policy in CI-LINT-01. Both existing workflows default to compilation/assembly, with release R8 and existing signing/publication safeguards. Full Lint is retained as the explicit `workflow_dispatch.full_lint` boolean, false by default; its original warning/dependency/error/report configuration and report-presence checks remain effective when selected. `lint.checkReleaseBuilds=false` prevents assembleRelease from reintroducing implicit Lint. No test task is enabled, and no baseline, downgraded diagnostic or successful-Lint claim is introduced for skipped analysis. A manual default run is also fast; a selected full-Lint run still blocks on actual errors.
- APK-SIZE-01: actual release assets are 4,184,374 bytes for 0.1.128 and 164,847,827 bytes for 0.1.131. Read the latter's ZIP directory and native entries without installing/executing the application. It contains no .onnx/.tflite/.safetensors/.pt weights. Native entries total 160,401,764 bytes: arm64-v8a 38,816,608; armeabi-v7a 26,920,928; x86 47,442,828; x86_64 47,221,400. All have ZIP_STORED (uncompressed), mostly ONNX Runtime and LiteRT. Set AGP 9.5 `packaging.jniLibs.useLegacyPackaging=true` so the direct-distribution APK compresses these libraries; preserve every ABI, full inference features and the existing signed update-manifest schema. No APK rewriting/re-signing, model bundling, architecture removal or dynamic native-code download. Installation will extract device-appropriate native libraries; do not equate download compression with a guaranteed decrease in installed storage. Measure the replacement artifact before quoting its actual size.
- UPDATE-NETWORK-01: screenshot 1000121540.jpg establishes an UnknownHostException while resolving github.com before the update request reached the server. Map DNS, timeout, connection and TLS failures to short actionable Chinese messages in both checks and downloads; do not print raw English host exceptions. Preserve the previous verified READY state if refresh fails, retaining its install action, and keep the last successful check time unchanged. Existing manual retry, partial-download continuation, canonical update hosts, manifest/APK signatures, SHA256 and version guards remain. This does not repair the phone's DNS or establish a device-side successful update.

References actually read: official AGP 9.5 [JniLibsPackaging](https://developer.android.com/reference/tools/gradle-api/9.5/com/android/build/api/dsl/JniLibsPackaging), its official 9.5.0 sources.jar, [application extractNativeLibs guidance](https://developer.android.com/guide/topics/manifest/application-element#extractNativeLibs), and [16KB packaging guidance](https://developer.android.com/guide/practices/page-sizes). Use the Gradle DSL only; no extra manifest or legacy bundle property is necessary for this APK-producing AGP 9.5 app. Runtime model files remain private, optional, verified downloads. Scope is two workflow conditions/inputs, the Lint/JNI packaging configuration, update error/state handling and these existing records. Source/diff/artifact inspection and independent read-only review only; no local build, tests, emulator, device automation, runtime/API probes or model execution. Submit latest main and confirm its CI trigger, without claiming the next artifact passed or its exact savings.

Start horizontal overflow at the latest dates. Fixed color levels are zero, positive under 15 minutes, 15–30, 30–60, and at least 60 minutes, using the app accent rather than forcing GitHub green. Tap a real day to show its saved time and work count; cross-year selection displays the year, with date actions for accessibility. Do not synthesize history, mutate stored time or use the busiest day to change all color meanings. Keep the existing cover-based work ranking and continue action. |

## Guided panel reading · 2026-11-13 (PANEL-01–08; source implemented, CI/device acceptance pending)

CI follow-up (PANEL-CI-01): run 43374723210 / 0.1.129 reached Kotlin compilation during the Lint prerequisite and failed because PanelCamera omitted the existing Coil 3 crossfade extension import and GuidedPanelReader omitted the ExperimentalLayoutApi opt-in for navigationBarsIgnoringVisibility. The remember/listener/throwable inference errors are downstream of the missing extension. Add those declarations only; preserve camera behavior, original-image decoding, native models and static-analysis gates. Missing Lint reports are secondary because analysis never ran. Source/log review and independent read-only review only; no local build or tests. Replacement main CI trigger is delivery evidence, not compilation or device acceptance.

Takeover request: “去接管漫画软件功能建议去做那个分镜功能”. Retrieved context for “漫画软件功能建议” (2026-11-13) retains “我们先做漫画分镜功能吧”, questions about realtime/preprocessing, image quality, model sizes and phone/PC feasibility, and the final choice “对的都弄进来才是合适的让用户自己抉择怎么开启以及开启哪个”. The retained context is an excerpt, not a new verbatim transcript of the whole discussion. Implement optional traditional, lightweight and higher-precision local detection with independently optional learned order; do not force one engine on every user.

| ID | Adopted behavior and source implementation |
| --- | --- |
| PANEL-01 | Reader settings disclose one “分镜阅读” switch, then Traditional / lightweight AI / high-precision AI, right-to-left / left-to-right order, optional learned order and model management. Defaults remain ordinary reading. Choices persist per manga through the existing preference/backup path; existing vertical, paged and spread layouts remain available when panel mode is off. Suppress spread-only controls while panel mode is active. Reuse the reader's actual Ovio MotionTabs, press-stretch ReaderToggle, MotionDialog and fixed PANEL/READING_DOCK glass roles. |
| PANEL-02 | Identify the current page on demand, pre-read no more than its next two pages, and persist only normalized coordinates in an expendable 128-page/8MiB/30-day cache. Source/page metadata, algorithm/model revision, engine and order own cache identity; local files additionally include size/mtime. Current-page changes cancel the old pre-read window. Analysis bitmap is bounded to 1536px, never persisted or substituted for the original display. |
| PANEL-03 | Traditional detection adapts MIT paneldet's XY gutter branch, with conservative retained-ink/area checks; ordering adapts Apache-2.0 Komai recursive XY cuts and spread separation. No uniform fake panel grid, whole-image preprocessing requirement or image recompression. Unreliable detection preserves the whole page and says so. Missing/failed selected models visibly fall back to traditional detection and are not cached as model success. |
| PANEL-04 | Lightweight engine is the pinned YOLO26 Nano INT8 TFLite (2,837,823 bytes); the deployable higher-precision choice is Koharu 1152 ONNX (148,442,003 bytes), explicitly named as that version. Optional Inkwell order is 103,294 bytes and operates on box geometry. Actual CPU runtime, input normalization, quantization, model class indexes/output buffers and order features are implemented, rather than placeholder engine choices. Native inference is serialized and bounded to two intra-op threads; old detector sessions close before changing engines. No mobile speed/accuracy equivalence is established. |
| PANEL-05 | Models download only on an explicit model-management action, using immutable revision URLs and exact byte counts/SHA256. Verified bytes install atomically; download progress, cancellation, retry, removal and source/license links are available. Closing settings leaves the explicit download running. Private model weights are excluded from Android backup and from the APK; LiteRT/ONNX Runtime native libraries are packaged. The existing source/auth request paths are not changed. |
| PANEL-06 | One Telephoto 0.16.0 ZoomableAsyncImage retains the original image and native region decoding. A finite 320ms camera owns both scale and center, can redirect to a newly selected panel and stops for manual gestures; reduced motion snaps. Full-page overview draws detector boxes in the image's actual coordinates and lets a tap select a panel. Edge taps and explicit previous/next actions move through panels; a deliberate single-finger left swipe advances after release, matching the existing app convention. Two-finger zoom remains available. |
| PANEL-07 | The original page remains the shared reader cursor. The existing chapter/history write queue stores optional panel index and layout signature, separate from vertical pixel offsets, with old-backup defaults. Resume clamps against real detected panels. A mode change clears incompatible panel selection; panel navigation clears the obsolete vertical offset. Image visibility, current-chapter progress protection, collection, page navigator, chapter controls and end comments remain on the existing reader path. |
| PANEL-08 | Analysis failure leaves the full page usable, offers re-identification and ordinary reading, and does not invent successful model output. Original-image failure owns an explicit single-page retry. Model completion/removal invalidates the active page's detection effect. Original per-source image headers, local/download resolution and error ownership are retained. |

Sources actually read for adaptation: [paneldet f0ef279](https://github.com/jsneij/paneldet/blob/f0ef27986969fce6ca2dd75e2be2962b56db376c/paneldet.py), [Komai f567e2b](https://github.com/erik-sandoval/komai/tree/f567e2be8f30b59f04d14dd7fc589e186fb72ff8/app/src/main/java/eu/kanade/tachiyomi/ui/reader/viewer/panel), pinned model cards/code identified in PanelModelStore and bundled under assets/licenses, and the exact Telephoto 0.16.0 APIs recorded in third_party/telephoto/ADAPTATION.md. Algorithm/model/runtime licenses remain separate; the Nano model is AGPL-3.0, the older Koharu 1152 export retains its custom/other source terms and Manga109 declaration, and Inkwell order is MIT. The recent HighRes 1920 safetensors model is a distinct candidate with no verified ready-to-deploy Android/ONNX artifact in the material read; it is not mislabeled as the shipped 1152 option. Its conversion/deployment remains an explicit follow-up, not a completed engine.

Implementation: reader/panels owns detection, downloads/native runtimes, camera and panel UI; ReaderScreen/ReaderSettingsPanel select that presentation and reuse existing callbacks; ReaderPreferences/LibraryStore persist optional settings/positions. No parallel reader activity or original-image store is added. Independent source review corrected stale disposal cursors, remembered spread grouping, leftover vertical offsets and the spread-only toolbar action. Existing main CI owns Lint, Kotlin/R8/package/sign/release. No local build, executable tests, emulator, UI/API probes, synthetic screenshots or real-device acceptance were performed. Source implementation does not establish compilation, quality on complex borderless/long-strip artwork, low-memory performance or gesture/visual acceptance; those remain pending the existing main artifact and user feedback.

It supersedes ACCOUNT-RELAY-02 / ACCOUNT-RETRY-03's hand-built password POST and App-profile requirement for new official-page sessions, without migrating existing sessions.

Evidence and limits: the current first-party freeSite bundle submits exactly six fields to its own origin's `/api/kb/web/login`; its normal account center reads `/api/v2/web/user/info`. Our former field encoding matched, but the HTTP client/browser session and post-login profile route differed. Neither that difference nor this change establishes the cause of the original 210. An authenticated official account center was actually observed in shared Chrome, including its nonsecret login-username field. The earlier failed username and later successful username differed; this is not a same-account/same-network comparison and is not MangaFlow login success. No credentials or raw authenticated responses are recorded here.

| ID | Implemented behavior | Completion boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ACCOUNT-WEBSITE-04 | Replace the copied native password submission with the official HTTPS account-password page inside the existing account screen. A real installed WebView executes the current official encoding, form validation, request, cookies and errors. The desktop route retains the installed engine's real version; only its device classification is adjusted to select the inspected `/web` contract. Small CSS/viewport overrides fit the actual `#loginV2` form to phone width and remove its duplicated website header/footer. No native password capture, saved password, automatic refill/replay, alternative endpoint or separate outward-browser entry. | The source no longer implements its own password encoding/POST. A WebView is still a different environment from desktop Chrome and the official Android App; do not claim their behavior is identical. Current Android form appearance and successful submission are unverified. |
| ACCOUNT-WEBSITE-BRIDGE-04 | Before loading, install AndroidX WebKit 1.14.0's exact-origin WebMessageListener/document-start observer. Only main-frame messages from `https://www.copy4000.com`, the active attempt/document nonce and newest actual POST submission sequence are eligible. The observer filters both original and final login URL, method, HTTP 200, business 200 and bounded token; it never reads request bodies/passwords/Cookies or forwards raw responses. Early XHR readyState observation allows the site's normal successful redirect; the fetch observer clones its response. | A bridge result is only a candidate. Repository session-generation/cancellation checks and a real profile request remain mandatory; no login is inferred from page navigation, token arrival or nickname text. |
| ACCOUNT-WEBSITE-SESSION-04 | Every pane uses a fresh isolated WebKit profile. Clear that profile's cookies, DOM storage, history/cache and WebView on success, close, route exit or app stop. Each cleanup step is attempted even if another fails. Credential handoff waits for the isolated profile's Cookie deletion callback; closing or stopping while that callback is pending invalidates delivery, and a bounded timeout reports failure. DOM-storage cleanup has no completion receipt and is recorded only as requested. Clear old orphan profile shells on the next available process; never reuse one for a new attempt. Check all required WebKit features; unsupported engines show an actionable update error without replaying through the old path. Deny non-HTTPS/foreign main-frame navigation, file/content access, mixed content, popup windows and certificate-error continuation. | Cookie deletion is asynchronous; a separate profile prevents late deletion affecting a new login. This does **not** promise browser data never reaches disk. If synchronous cleanup fails at credential handoff, stop before saving and report the failure. |
| ACCOUNT-WEBSITE-PROFILE-04 | A new encrypted `website` channel verifies `/api/v2/web/user/info` on the same exact www origin, validates account identity, and only then commits. Retain the existing memory-only, five-minute profile-retry candidate and stale-response guards. Save only the nonsecret form/handoff mode across configuration changes so an empty reopened form does not erase a pending verification candidate. Saved WEB/APP/WEB_API credentials retain their old verification routes. Preserve 209/210/429 error status when propagating profile failures. | Verification still uses the owner HTTP client. Other owner collection/history/readback operations deliberately continue through the existing signed account API; the whole owner chain is **not** claimed to equal the website. Public catalog/images/reviews remain anonymous. |

Official source inspected: `https://s3.mangafunb.fun/static/websitefree/loginStatic/js/app.75df92b0c2ef8fdb5b8e.js` (SHA256 `2d43c3c04d080584a60993a95a8b6bea52a717b7ec652f896e61b12c2f537365`) and `person.1b8bbe2f18a97f2437e5.js`; source was read, not copied into the app. Android primary references: `https://developer.android.com/reference/androidx/webkit/WebViewCompat`, `/ProfileStore`, `/Profile`, and the official WebKit 1.14.0 release entry. Existing AccountScreen/MangaMotion components own native loading/error/retry/import states; the default password form is the original official implementation. No new visual acceptance is asserted.

Scope: account browser bridge and pane, profile/channel routing, ViewModel/UI wiring and the pinned WebKit dependency. ## Drawer interaction continuity · 2026-11-13 (DRAWER-CONTINUITY-16)

Coroutine cancellation/rapid reversal clears only its own request by identity, so a cancelled selection cannot navigate later or erase a newer request. Normal navigation/deep-link requests invalidate pending drawer selection. Reduced-motion programmatic actions snap.
- The drawer already supplies the spatial transition. Its root-to-root handoff uses an immediate content replacement after closing, avoiding a second full-page entrance and avoiding destination composition during close. All non-drawer root and detail/reader transitions keep the current navigation motion and saved-state keys.
- Back and underlying route/input gates use actual drawer presence through opening, dragging and the final closing frame, rather than only isOpen. Outgoing menu content stops accepting input; manual drag and native scrim dismissal retain native ownership and cancel a superseded programmatic action.
- Keep the root's mangaHazeSource node attached. Separate the drawer's decorative frosted effect from its always-mounted foreground and compose that effect only while the drawer is present over a root. Official Haze 2.1 HazeSourceNode draw() releases its layer and draws content directly without capture demand; Haze.kt's public source API has no enabled flag. This removes app-level source modifier churn while retaining demand-driven recording and no active drawer consumer when closed. - DOCK-11: use one bounded liquid dock at 220dp wide / 52dp minimum height, condensing to 140dp / 44dp; scale its width for larger text within parent constraints. ## Full Haze 2.1 runtime migration · 2026-11-13 (HAZE-21-01, implemented; CI/device acceptance pending)

User correction, recorded before implementation: “完整引入最新 2.1 的全部能力啊你在搞什么？”. This supersedes GLASS-PIPELINE-07's decision to retain Haze 1.6 and copy selected shader formulas. The goal is the complete official 2.1.0 core, Blur, Glass, Material3 and material-preset runtime, including native container fields/member transitions, semantic and progressive blur, full optical/detail/rim stages, interaction responses, typed inputs/retention, source sharing, accessibility, performance profiles and the eligible native Android backdrop backend. Do not describe a source subset or merely adding unused dependencies as completion.

Preserve the fixed liquid/frosted/plain role map, latest detail reading layout, three-segment header at rest, persistent measured selector anchor/label alignment, source-icon stack and current M2 gestures. Delete the superseded app optical renderer and hand-built two-member shader field. Use official style/node/container APIs and real page sources; only application role, layout and input-policy glue remains. Upgrade the necessary toolchain to upstream-compatible AGP 9.5.0 / Kotlin 2.4.21 / Gradle 9.8.2 / Compose BOM 2026.09.00 / compileSdk 37, retaining Java 17, minSdk 26 and targetSdk 35. CI install/signing tool paths must agree. No tests/local build; main-only and CI-trigger-only delivery remain.

| Full SDK capability | Current application integration |
| --- | --- |
| Complete artifacts | Official `haze`, `haze-blur`, `haze-blur-material3`, `haze-blur-materials`, `haze-glass`, `haze-glass-material3`, all 2.1.0. No copied or reduced app optical shaders remain. Upstream source reference is tag 2.1.0 / `ca21ff2060e52e1882afdb94f9b055f7fc834bd9`. |
| Full Glass rendering | `LiquidGlassSurface` applies official `hazeGlass` with Regular/Clear and Material3. SDK owns responsive optical size, semantic depth/blur, sharp refracted detail, normals, Fresnel, colour, rim, clipping, retained output and supported-platform fallback. Expansion changes authored strength/displacement/tint in a remembered node-observed style. No 44dp/60dp optical-reference freeze, delayed size writeback, own padding or extra shader pass. |
| Containers and member transitions | `GlassControlGroup` uses official `hazeGlassContainer` and native `hazeGlass` members, not app SDF arrays. Rest uses zero merge distance to retain segments; a shared finite PressInteraction from real member hit regions enables 16dp merging. Native matched-geometry member transitions, grouped-member support and reduced-motion policy remain available. The left menu stays outside this container. Pointer glue does not occupy the transparent gap or consume gestures. |
| Typed input and native backend | Navigation/reading docks use `HazeInput.Backdrop(state)` with official source fallback. Panel/popover/drawer/artwork/gallery/nested controls use explicit Sources so another window or another glass surface is never mistaken for the intended input. The gallery atmosphere uses `HazeInput.Content` semantic blur. `HazeFeatureFlags.isPlatformBackdropEnabled` is configured in Application before modifiers attach; native window rendering requires a supported Android 37.2+ hardware window, otherwise the full SDK's portable source renderer runs. |
| Retention / source sharing | Sources normally keep last output across a transient absence; the drawer explicitly clears unavailable output. `mangaHazeSource` uses SDK Auto input sharing on normal-memory Android and Never on low-RAM devices, with explicit 32dp overflow outsets. Haze owns demand, recording, caches, source/window coordinates and release. This is a memory policy, not a measured performance claim. |
| Blur / materials / progression | Fixed frosted roles use official Blur, Material3 and HazeMaterials presets, with zero Glass lens. The gallery uses the SDK's progressive optical diffusion and content blur. Material presets (including the module's Cupertino/Fluent alternatives) are available without a new skin chooser or restoring a user-visible material switch. |
| Interaction / accessibility / quality | SDK hover/focus/press styles and MaterialOnly scaling respond through each native modifier; reduced motion removes decorative responses and snaps container motion. Root providers map Android UI contrast, preserve upstream reduce-transparency/border preferences, use the app's opaque theme fallback and protect frosted readability. Quality is used normally; low-RAM uses Balanced. No idle animation loop, CPU pixel readback or renderer-specific public settings. |

Toolchain sources: Haze's fixed version catalog/wrapper and AAR minCompileSdk=37.0 checks; Android's AGP 9.5 compatibility/built-in Kotlin guidance; official Google Maven BOM 2026.09.00 (Android Compose 1.12.1, Material3 1.4.0, icons 1.7.8); Gradle 9.8.2 official distribution checksum. AGP built-in Kotlin uses an explicit 2.4.21 buildscript classpath; the obsolete Kotlin Android plugin and kotlinOptions block are removed. Both workflows compile/package only and use SDK 37 plus Build Tools 36.0.0, including signing paths.

Evidence is actual source/API/metadata review and the published main/CI trigger. No local build, tests, emulator, screenshot automation, device frame timings or Apple pixel-equivalence claim. Runtime fidelity, native Android 37.2 backend, source-sharing memory cost and actual interaction acceptance remain user-device observations.

Replace Material IconButton's independent ripple with the existing shared press interaction; the actual lens and icon remain one group. Reserve visible rear edges before sizing its complete front mark; retain all hidden sources instead of stopping at the first one that fits. Overlap the actual marks, with transparent start cutouts so rear letters do not run through the foreground wordmark. Rear glyph boxes align to the exposed end before clipping, preventing a narrow reveal from containing only transparent icon-slot padding; RTL mirrors both alignment and cutout. There are no colored plates, white wallpaper masks, borders, generic substitutes or +N. Full real values remain available to accessibility and the existing tap disclosure. Extremely narrow remaining room yields to the required heat, with all source details still disclosed. Library comparison used the retained toast-01 case's complete-front / receding-edge grouping principle; this is a bounded adjustment to the existing Kotlin stack, not a claimed Sonner or avatar-component source port.

| GLASS-COST-07 | Each surface owns its mutable shader uniforms and caches its effect/depth input. Zero-pressure/far-away lights do not enter the effect cache key. Morph shadow strength tints a white cached A8 mask, avoiding colour-only mask regeneration. Capture padding has a fixed authored maximum; older devices keep stronger .64/.72 minimum frosted tint and .82/.88 fallback alpha. | No idle frame loop, screenshot analysis, new dependencies, tests or local build. It adds no translation backend/model, automatic requests, dependency, permission or simulated returned text; inline translation remains unimplemented.

Reuse: existing Sakurairo-inspired fixed-art/nested-scroll relationship, the already attributed Ovio MotionTabs/MotionSwap and shared press adaptation, current Haze 1.6/Haze Glass role presets, local chapter history/download records and native translation helper. ## Apple-reference material rework · 2026-11-13 (active)

User: “没达到我们审美库里保存的那个苹果液态玻璃效果啊”. This rejects visual closure of GLASS-MATERIAL-04 / GLASS-LIGHT-04; their opacity/input fixes are historical, not acceptance of the material. - GLASS-OPTICS-05: adapt the archived Haze Glass 2.1.0 optical/rim formulas at `ca21ff2060e52e1882afdb94f9b055f7fc834bd9`, keeping the existing Haze 1.6 backdrop transport. Replace the old Backdrop edge-only shader, rather than stack both engines. Capture a padded sharp backdrop and compose sharp/blur depth before edge refraction, Fresnel ambient response, gamut-bounded colour grading and a normal-lit rim. Keep foreground labels outside this pipeline. Regular navigation/docks and Clear compact controls retain different authored optics. Large reading panels remain calmer.
- GLASS-INPUT-05: keep existing Ovio/morph motion and raw-pointer cancellation. Localized press light and refraction follow the pointer without consuming it; interaction-source presses without a pointer use the centre. Reduced motion removes that response. No additional whole-container scaling or new layout.
- GLASS-BOUNDARY-05: preserve all screenshot reversals, chapter/translation/page-collection changes and concurrent performance work. Keep the frosted and unsupported/software/no-source fallbacks. This is an Android source adaptation, not Apple’s renderer or the full Haze 2.1 runtime. Native Android blur substitutes for the upstream semantic blur; merged material bridges and sharp edge-detail overlays are outside this slice. Do not claim equivalent device appearance without user feedback.

Retain bounded readability as an optical white-point response to sampled luminance, rather than a separate wash/inner plate. Dark appearance must lower bright manga behind white ink; Clear controls must lift dark artwork behind dark ink. These coefficients are app tuning, not measured accessibility results.

## Motion system v2 implemented · 2026-11-13 (current)

User first requested “去对我们现在的 uiux 动画动效系统进行升级优化先去好好设计下”, then authorized the complete proposed design with “去交给你完全全部落地”. The seven-page `MangaFlow_Motion_Design_20261113.pdf` is the proposal; this section owns the adopted implementation and its limits. Extend MOTION-01–11 and the existing Ovio/beUI/Vaul/Backdrop adaptations, retaining the latest detail/carousel reversals and concurrent PERF-06–09 / GLASS-MATERIAL-04 corrections. No new animation runtime or dependency is introduced.

| ID | Adopted implementation / actual source | Observable acceptance and boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| M2-01 | ExpandingSearchBar starts icon and shell together. A measured, fixed-width text row requests focus once its visible text region reaches min(96dp, target text width) and its window is focused. Real results retain their own height. IME owns the first back; a later back previews the search shell, with finite foreground exit and existing hidden-input bounds. | Opening does not wait for the former 70ms lead or 90%/98% completion thresholds. Closing/reversing retains one X and restores the original header without an invisible full-width foreground. Keyboard appearance timing itself remains platform-owned. |
| M2-02 | Keep Ovio's independent leading/trailing edges, selected ranges, text weight reservation and per-pane state. Shared pane entries use the navigation spring, soft fades use 200/140ms, and at most six ranking entrants retain 18ms selection-only staggering. Audience press uses the common .94 icon amplitude. | Rapid selection retargets the present geometry; refreshes do not replay the list or reset positions. Existing male/female depth exchange and full period labels remain. |
| M2-03 | Ordinary route and cover entries share k=520, damping=1 navigation physics; navigation opacity is 160/120ms. Keep placement-specific cover identity, image placeholders and saved source positions. | A cache/image response does not retrigger a route or match another placement of the same manga. Existing sharedElement/sharedBounds distinctions remain. |
| M2-04 | MotionBackHandler uses Activity 1.10.1 PredictiveBackHandler. Each AnimatedContent key owns one MotionBackState shared by its handler/drawing and ReaderScreen. System progress maps to <=8% viewport travel, <=3% scale reduction and a bounded corner change. Completion calls the real close action once immediately; cancellation springs back. Signed origin mapping lets a new gesture catch a cancel return from either edge. | No model navigation, chapter switch or save is committed during preview/cancel. Completing uses the retained route exit. During a predictive gesture the ordinary shared-cover overlay is suppressed so it cannot jump into untransformed coordinates; normal buttons/three-button back retain the cover flight. This is a deliberate adaptation of the current root, not a seekable shared-element implementation or claim that the platform lacks one. |
| M2-05 | Reader tools use 180ms, 12dp overlay entrances with existing fixed system insets and release/cancel seek semantics. Measure the actual lower control host, remove its already-accounted navigation inset and pass the real clearance into ChapterEndComments. Navigator/scrub/modal states suppress previews. | Long titles, font scale and actual action rows do not overlap the comment entry. Tool visibility does not resize/scale the manga or change saved page/offset. Preserve the concurrent tiled long-strip reader. |
| M2-06 | BottomPullLoad retains 96dp, genuine pointer release, final-image/base-zoom arbitration, circular geometry and request ownership. A pointer VelocityTracker supplies only bounded release momentum; the existing feedback returns with k=420, damping=.86. Threshold haptics rearm below .88 instead of at every tiny crossing. | A fast fling cannot request a chapter. Multi-touch/cancellation/reversal do not dispatch. Progress still represents actual distance or the real loading state. |
| M2-07 | SwipeToRemove preserves 55% left-drag release commitment, reset-on-submit and existing undo/storage ownership. Regrabbing catches the returning drawing; cancellation returns with bounded velocity. Threshold haptics rearm below .47. BookshelfBook similarly springs an unaccepted/canceled drag to its original slot; the actual trash target alone decides removal. | An immediate undo cannot resurrect a half-swiped row. No deletion waits on an animation callback. Keyed history/favorite exits and placement respect disabled motion. Trash feedback animates only color/icon, keeping the circular drop geometry fixed. |
| M2-08 | Shared press physics retain beUI k=833/damping=.866, with .94 icons and .985 broad surfaces. Extract the already adopted Settings press-stretch thumb into MotionSwitchThumb and reuse it in ReaderToggle: k=300, damping=.72, 18→21dp thumb width. | Full accessible switch rows stay stable; text does not inherit a moving switch thumb's shape. RTL and the saved software palette remain. No extra press transform is added around an existing transformed surface. |
| M2-09 | Retain existing local long-comment/reply/filter disclosures and fixed icon swapping. Source selection uses color feedback without scaling its text; source/filter touch rows are at least 44dp. | Selection does not move adjacent text or replay the feed. Existing source identity, compact translation and real replies stay intact. |
| M2-10 | ChapterEndComments retains stable per-comment flight parameters and passthrough input. Keep <=3 simultaneous bubbles, 3.6–4.4s flight and 1.2–1.6s admission intervals. Static single preview is used for disabled motion/touch exploration; no decorative timer runs in that mode. Existing resumed-only work and modal disposal remain. | Previews are staggered without covering the measured controls, intercepting scroll, auto-reading or exposing spoilers. Full original text remains in the comment sheet. |
| M2-11 | Existing retained panel presence/input gates stay authoritative. CompactGlassPopover computes its transform origin from the actual clamped popup position and trigger centre, including automatic above/below placement. | The same trigger opens/closes from a coherent direction near every screen edge; leaving contents do not accept actions. Native sheet/dialog focus and dismissal remain. |
| M2-12 | Preserve the actual Material multi-browse carousel, restored dots, keyed placements and current content-retaining refresh. Its existing touch/modal/resumed/reduced-motion guards remain; the hand-written discovery refresh loop now also runs only while resumed. | Returning/refreshing does not replay stable content; background decorative rotation does not keep running. |
| M2-13 | Existing MotionSwap slots and real download progress remain. Common MotionBusyIndicator covers indeterminate busy views across reading, browse, history, account, settings, downloads and restore UI; disabled motion draws a static semantic busy ring. Determinate overloads retain the platform's actual progress, colors and tracks. | Loading never fabricates completion or changes action-slot width. Existing delayed-busy feedback delays presentation only, never the result or business action. |
| M2-14 | Continue the system animator preference and platform-owned clock. Finite drawing transforms remain in graphicsLayer/draw callbacks; cancel-return frames do not recompose swipe contents. Fix the explicit shelf/trash animation gaps when motion is disabled. Preserve shared Haze capture, new glass role presets and caches. | No added per-cover blur, whole-reader content scaling on scroll, duplicated time scaling or required wait for decorative completion. Disabled effects retain all controls, actual progress and accessible state. |

Implementation integration: the original review base was e9b80b2; subsequent main 46ad627's asynchronous restoration, chapter-first preparation, long-strip region decoding and glass material corrections were merged without replacing their source, dependencies or notices. Further concurrent main changes must be incorporated before publication.

Source-only review found and corrected three re-entry issues before publication: mismatched route-back state lifetimes, stale swipe offsets on immediate undo, and a zero-origin jump when interrupting predictive-back cancellation. The current diff was reviewed independently against the source and documented API signatures. No tests, local builds, lint, API probes, simulator, UI automation, benchmarks or device verification were run. Direct latest main with observed main-CI trigger is the delivery boundary; compilation, visual motion and phone hand feel remain unverified until their respective results are observed.

References: the fixed Ovio `81a1bcb` / beUI `a866cb2` sources and licenses already listed under Motion system; Android official `animation/customize`, `animation/advanced`, `animation/shared-elements`, `animation/quick-guide` and `system/predictive-back-progress`; current application Compose BOM 2025.05.01 and Activity 1.10.1. New parameter values are project tuning, not official constants or measured frame-rate claims.

## Remaining performance work implemented · 2026-11-13 (current)

User follow-up to the four remaining items: “那赶紧全部落地”. Scope is startup restoration, chapter-first download preparation, isolated download publications and long-strip region decoding. The existing reader gestures, original image indexes, per-chapter history, explicit download intent and latest restored UI remain the acceptance baseline.

| ID | Implementation | Observable acceptance / evidence boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| PERF-06 | LibraryStore now opens preferences and parses saved collections/history/settings on IO. A ready/error stream distinguishes unrestored state from an empty library. Early writes are replayed against restored records; reading/settings wait for readiness and the library uses its quiet loading/retry treatment. Backup export/restore and chapter-history reads await the same owner. | Discovery can draw while personal data restores. An early favorite/progress action cannot replace saved data with an empty snapshot. Resume reads the restored chapter/offset and settings. Readiness is not proof of successful disk persistence. |
| PERF-07 | DownloadStore can load one requested manifest through its chapter mutex without waiting for global initialization. The initial file index reads existence/length, then image-header validation and scheduler reconciliation run on IO. Readers validate their requested page; workers prepare only their chapter before transfer. Generation and per-page write versions protect concurrent replacements. | Opening an online/saved chapter does not wait for every downloaded image's bounds to decode. Nonempty files alone never let a new transfer declare completion: all pages must belong to a validated generation. Pause/delete/refresh retain ownership; damaged files retain the existing repair/error flow. Startup verification updates its in-memory index without rewriting every manifest. |
| PERF-08 | Download status is collected in the current detail, reader chapter and mounted chapter dialog, rather than at the application root. The shared store exposes filtered/distinct flows. Progress saves reuse the stable ID order and sort again only for membership/creation-time changes. | Another manga's download does not invalidate the reading/application root. Current progress, chapter marks and download management still update. Initial cached values avoid clearing download marks during subscription handoff. |
| PERF-09 | Long vertical pages over two viewport heights retain their bounded Coil preview and overlay fixed source bands using Telephoto 0.16.0's actual region decoder. Only visible bands plus one adjacent band on either side are composed; original files/cache snapshots stay pinned for that page. The public custom-source adapter translates crop coordinates while the upstream engine owns sampling, EXIF and decoder pooling. | Detailed regions use cached/downloaded original bytes with no second GET or extra disk copy. Bands remain inside the original page item, keep its fitted aspect/offset/count, and add no touch gesture owner. Unsupported region formats or evicted cache files retain the existing preview. Source geometry/resource review is not measured phone smoothness or visual acceptance. |

Implementation source map: `third_party/telephoto/ADAPTATION.md`. Source/diff review plus independent read-only review covered startup writes, lazy restoration, worker error handling and premature completion. No tests, benchmarks, local builds, API probes, simulator or device automation were run. Deliver directly on latest main, confirm CI triggered, and leave real-device acceptance to the user.

Long menus/panels diffuse more and refract less than small controls. | Retain backdrop colour/structure without a coordinate-based inner white plate. Remove the liquid mode's uniform dark outline; keep the existing diffuse exterior shadow. | Navigation, search, reader controls and floating menus share the same material owner. Preserve shared Press/DragInteraction ownership and the existing reduced-motion setting. | Glass observes without consuming input. Scrolling must not leave the material pressed; slider/button interaction signals can remain active through their own source. Cancellation returns the directional light/strength to rest. |

## UI/UX code review follow-up · 2026-11-13 (UIUX-REVIEW-01)

User explicitly requested an implementation follow-up: “去看现在的代码看看 uiux 还有什么能优化的” followed by “去全部落地”. Preserve the previous screenshot reversals, stable data semantics and existing Ovio/Backdrop/beUI adaptations; this batch is a concrete source refinement rather than a replacement design system.

- Shelf: switch the flat collection from row-first keys to item-stable manga IDs in a lazy grid, preserving covers, title/progress labels, swipe gestures and drag-to-trash. Favorite/history deletion hides the selected record, offers an undo Snackbar and commits only after dismissal. No download files are touched.
- Download: keep grouping by manga ID and exact chapter transfer semantics. Use the normal source-backed cover loader for online works, retaining the original local-import cover path. Tighten complete-record chapter/page/size metadata into one band when space allows. Do not infer a lost volume or manipulate stored entries from a screenshot-sized capacity difference.
- Reader: isolate the active chapter's download state to avoid unrelated queue emissions invalidating reader chrome. Memoize 512 intrinsic image dimensions by page URL, using existing stored/disk dimensions before 0.7 placeholders. Preserve the concurrent PERF-03/04 collectLatest/window enabled and idle-deferred resizing, all prefetch caps, exact chapter/page/offset and download recovery.
- Detail: leave the restored immersive cover layout intact, soften only the local dark gradient and remove redundant single-group chapter count. Rename the existing immediate-download mode honestly, without pretending it batches selections. Keep separate download marks and per-chapter reading progress.
- Recent reading/statistics: show compact actual page progress rather than pulling full source scores into history rows. The fourteen-day chart selects real day and duration on tap, with an active-day summary when lifetime and fortnight totals are identical. Retain exact time data.
- Panels: shorten the shared bottom-sheet drag handle, picture collection previews and spacing; keep popup access, scrolling, image jump and non-destructive accessibility. Set disclosure measurements to zero while fully closed but keep their composed children alive so wallpaper imports are not canceled.
- Motion/performance: use the existing search/list curves for comment text expansion and a lower-radius homepage background blur; maintain native carousel, Ovio segmented-edge motion, cover shared bounds, theming and reduced-motion controls.

Evidence boundary: static source and difference review, not measured speed or device visual verification. Do not write/run tests, local builds, benchmarks or API probes. Commit directly to the latest main with all concurrent changes retained and confirm the main CI workflow was triggered. Installation and interaction acceptance remain with the user.

All existing score providers, attribution, native values and list layout remain. Background subscription cancellation does not remove already returned scores. |

A changed nonblank latest-chapter label, a missing requested continuation chapter or explicit retry requires a source refresh. A cold open reads the persisted full catalog before revalidation, and entering the reader during an active detail request joins that background catalog work. | Cached chapters stay usable while refreshing or offline. Keep the existing four-request bound and original-byte disk cache. | Rapid forward/back movement prioritizes the current window. Pausing the app/settings does not discard a visible image's existing request or start another background candidate. Resuming re-emits the loaded visible position so next-chapter prefetch can recover without another swipe. |
| PERF-04 | Wait for the vertical list to stop dragging/flinging before applying late original aspect ratios. Preserve already-known dimensions, original-page positions and chapter-end layout readiness. | A late size response cannot resize the page during a moving gesture. Leaving/replacing a page cancels its wait. Post-idle layout, very long strips and restored offsets remain pending device feedback. |
| PERF-05 | Remember spread construction independently of the current page; collect reader stores with lifecycle awareness. Keep the last chapter-position map while loading a newer revision instead of briefly resetting all progress marks to unknown. | Page changes do not rebuild all spread groups; history updates do not clear every chapter mark before the replacement snapshot arrives. Existing per-chapter positions, manual spread correction and recent-reading protection remain. |

Reference: the existing Coil 3.2.0 / AndroidX lifecycle 2.9.0 and Compose state APIs. Coil's pinned `NetworkFetcher.Factory` already retains one lazy network client; source review ruled out a per-image client allocation, so the image-loader setup is unchanged. Source/diff review and an independent read-only review are the evidence for this batch. No tests, local build, benchmarks, API probes or device/visual checks were run; no frame-rate or latency improvement has been measured. ## Per-chapter reading history and accidental-open protection · 2026-11-12 (current)

User: “关于历史记录我希望是做个每一话的记录这个会不会很耗费磁盘？因为我有时候不小心点到其他话就把我最新的记录覆盖了”. Exact message ID unavailable. Source inspection finds that LibraryStore already retains each chapter's page/count/outer offset, but the visible history and continue target contain only one latest record per manga. Initial pager/list saves also immediately replace that target.

| ID | Current decision and implementation | Acceptance boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| HISTORY-CHAPTER-01 | Add a named history icon to the existing library-history and recent-reading rows. Reuse MotionBottomSheet, the compact two-line list and MotionTabContent's retained-target transition; recent-reading opens chapter records inside the same sheet and returns to its retained book-list scroll. Each row names the actual chapter, original page/count and last saved time, and continues through the existing per-chapter restore path. | Read only local progress and the checked cached directory on IO. No network/cover downloads to browse history. Old records without titles can use their cached directory; unknown dates remain “早期记录”, with no fabricated chronology. No nested sheet stack or independent reading path. |
| HISTORY-RESUME-01 | Save every chapter independently while separating promotion of the per-comic continue target. An initial restore/automatic save in a different chapter cannot immediately replace an existing target. Actual user paging/scrolling/page navigation or three seconds of eligible RESUMED visible reading confirms the new chapter. Use the existing lifecycle/activity owner and verify manga/chapter identity in the callback. | Loading, background, hidden reader and settings/catalog/comment overlays cannot qualify the timer. Old-child disposal may flush its own chapter but cannot displace a newer confirmed chapter. The current primary chapter still saves its latest page, first-ever reading gets an entry, and explicit restart retains its intended reset. This limits brief accidental opens; every old chapter remains reachable even after a later deliberate switch. |
| HISTORY-STORAGE-01 | Extend the existing compact ChapterProgress JSON with optional bounded chapter title and timestamp. Keep one latest position per chapter, share the manga metadata, and reuse the background coalescing writer and original keys. The local chapter-history query snapshots pending/clear/removal state, then parses away from the reader lock. | No images, page bodies, duplicated full manga object, new database, dependency or ever-growing entry for every visit. Old JSON and backup version 1 remain readable; the prior latest entry is retained before switching chapters. Clear/remove/backup restoration invalidate the chapter-history view through a revision, preserving the writer's generation/removal ordering. |

Storage sizing is an estimate from the compact metadata shape and SharedPreferences/XML overhead: typical per-chapter records are a few hundred bytes, so ten thousand chapters are generally on the order of a few MB (titles/IDs/platform overhead vary). The previous per-chapter positions already consume part of that; this change mainly adds names/time and access to them. This is not a measured device-storage benchmark. Actual image caches/downloads remain separate.

Reuse: the existing LibraryStore progress writer, ChapterPages restore/navigation state, lifecycle-owned RecordReaderActivity, checked ReaderCatalogStore and adopted motion/sheet/list controls. Source/diff review covers old/new keys, pending saves, clear/remove/restore, reader ownership and continuation. No tests, local build, emulator, API probe or UI automation. Direct main CI builds/releases; the chapter list, accidental-open feel and actual device storage remain for user feedback.

| READER-END-02 | Horizontal single/double-page reading uses physical left drag on the original final image to enter the next chapter, with a left arrow and “左滑进入下一话” hint. Reuse the existing distance/release state and pager nested-scroll chain; vertical reading keeps upward pull. | Start only on the settled, loaded final image, at base zoom; a gesture that merely arrives at the last page cannot also skip it. Implemented in the shared BottomPullLoad state/indicator, ReaderScreen and ChapterEndFooter. Source/diff and independent read-only review confirmed equal-axis scaling, physical nested-scroll direction before pager reversal, down-time eligibility, release/cancellation behavior and per-image Telephoto 0.16.0 display/zoom/animation guards (both images for a spread). Original vertical loading and pager image counts remain. No tests, local build or automatic UI validation; direct main and CI-trigger-only delivery remain. Circular appearance and horizontal gesture feel require user device feedback.
