- The same shell contains the input and, only for a nonblank query, the real results. An empty query has only the compact search capsule. Remove the upstream input divider. Preserve query caches, per-query positions, cancellation, error/retry and original manga data.
- Let the root search and results sample the actual toolbar/browse source as foreground siblings. Preserve `0a99fec`'s global glass skin and pinned Backdrop optics: use the clear control role for an empty input and the readable panel role for query results, with restrained floating elevation. Share the input's interaction source with the separate optical shell; icon press transforms remain independent. Keep text outside the optical layer; do not duplicate cover art or include the glass in its own sampling source.
- Back, outside click, clear and close keep one dismissal path. Leaving content loses interaction/focus immediately and remains only until its exit finishes; rapid reversal uses the current animation value. Focus/IME waits for the revealed input, only the overlay uses IME insets, and the drawer remains available. Respect reduced motion.

Scope is the discovery search and necessary host/results integration. Other concurrent reader, ranking, provider and glass-skin work must be preserved. Review source/diff, submit directly to current main and confirm CI trigger; no tests or local prebuild. Android appearance, keyboard timing and gesture feel remain pending user device feedback.

## Vertical reader scroll repair · 2026-11-12

User feedback: “看漫画设置成纵向滑动总是卡手”. Recorded from this conversation; no message ID or exact feedback timestamp was supplied.

- **READER-SCROLL-01 — Give ordinary vertical drags to the reading list.** Use Telephoto's `ContentScale.Fit` inside the existing aspect-ratio item, keeping top alignment and the existing pinch/double-tap zoom. `FillWidth` could leave the original image slightly taller than the rounded item, particularly when the item's ratio came from a rounded preview. Telephoto 0.16.0 accepts that small pan range and can consume the rest of the drag after reaching its edge. The reference is the pinned `saket/telephoto` commit `2a5e5f502605367c0f497bbbdc3804a15f5e2f74`, `RealZoomableState.canConsumePanChange`, `internal/contentPlacement.kt` and `internal/transformable.kt`; this is a source-based explanation, not device profiling.
- **READER-SCROLL-02 — Keep image geometry stable during scrolling.** Read original dimensions, including EXIF rotation, from an existing local file or Coil disk snapshot on IO. The bounded adjacent-page prefetch records these dimensions before the image enters the list; reuse them for layout and page recycling. If a visible image's size arrives late, apply the height change only after the drag/fling ends. Preview dimensions remain a fallback when original bytes cannot be inspected. No extra network request, bitmap allocation for measurement, automatic download or separate image cache is introduced. The explicit ExifInterface 1.4.1 dependency matches the version already used by Telephoto 0.16.0.
- **READER-SCROLL-03 — Do not wait for download persistence on the UI thread.** Move reading-page priority updates to IO with cancellation checked after obtaining the store lock. Next-chapter prefetch checks the published download snapshot instead of acquiring that lock. Keep the download transaction, user pause/delete intent and page-transfer sharing unchanged.

Preserve the chapter-end release gesture, image page counts, saved offsets, long strips, horizontal/double-page reading and existing image retry. A placeholder is not proof of reaching the chapter end: the last image and visible image layouts must have applied their dimensions before showing chapter-end previews or accepting a pull release. The release-time boundary predicate checks this directly. The bottom-pull observer does not consume ordinary body scrolling and is unchanged. The original-sized layout and gesture fit repair address different failure paths; neither source inspection nor a CI trigger proves phone smoothness. Review source/diffs, submit directly to current main and confirm the existing CI/CD trigger. No tests, local build, emulator or device automation; compilation and user hand-feel remain unverified at submission.

## Automatic list ratings and cache recovery · 2026-11-12

User: “不行啊你这个我都清理缓存了再打开还是只有拷贝的数据然后点击刷新才慢慢一点点冒出来也不是全部出来。。。” Screenshot `1000121374.jpg` shows the reported ranking before state. No chat message ID was supplied. This corrects SOURCE-02's former detail-first loading rule for additional providers; it does not change the existing badge layout, native scales or strict identity matching.

| ID | Decision and implementation | Observable requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| METRICS-01 | Visible manga request all nine score providers from `ReviewProviders.metricIds`, using identity/score requests without comment pages. Independent source queues replace the two-whole-manga bottleneck; confirmed aliases and cross-site IDs are shared within the lookup, separately from the full comment cache. | Opening a cold or cached ranking starts available external ratings automatically. Bangumi, AniList, MangaUpdates, MyAnimeList, Kitsu, MangaDex, Tencent, Bahamut and Manhuagui do not require a prior detail visit. A slow source does not block successful independent sources; unresolved identities and absent scores remain absent. |
| METRICS-02 | Browse refresh explicitly refreshes ratings for retained visible manga, bypassing local score TTLs while respecting real source rate limits. Cache clearing invalidates old work, keeps active subscriptions, and restarts them after clearing. A short departure grace avoids cancelling and requeueing the same manga during view transitions. | Stable row IDs cannot suppress an explicit rating refresh. Clearing disposable metadata does not strand a mounted card or erase saved provider associations. Do not prefetch every row in the list or restart requests for ordinary score recomposition. |
| METRICS-03 | Remove the provider-wide five-minute cooldown previously caused by any single book's failure. Keep failure backoff per manga/source, preserve old successful scores on transport failure, and shorten negative matches. Source-level bounded concurrency, deadlines, HTTP pacing and Retry-After remain in force. | One failed book cannot mute that provider for the rest of the ranking. Already received scores survive cancellation and cache reuse; concurrent results cannot overwrite newer complete snapshots. No fake scores or first-search-result binding. |

Source review identified the hard-coded two-provider due list, whole-manga queue, serial provider calls, missing refresh wiring and missing cache-clear subscription replay. The screenshot does not prove that tapping refresh originally started the ratings: the old in-flight queue could also finish afterward. Ranking rows already show all fetched scores; the compact-cover `+N` rule was not the cause. Delivery evidence is source/diff review and the main CI trigger only; actual cold-start latency, provider availability and device results remain pending user feedback.

## Glass skins · 2026-11-12

User: “弄到我们项目里要不做个关于玻璃皮肤的切换你打算怎么弄？” This follows the supplied reader screenshots and the request to research usable liquid-glass implementations. This slice introduces one global material choice and supersedes READER-GLASS-01's fixed blur and opaque-dialog material. Existing reading, chapter-only discussion and explicitly requested download behavior remain in place. No message ID was supplied; implementation is authorized, while the new appearance remains pending user feedback.

| ID | Decision and implementation | Observable requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GLASS-SKIN-01 | Add 毛玻璃 / 液态玻璃 to Settings → 外观 and reader settings using the existing Ovio-derived `MotionTabs`. Persist one `GlassSkin` field in `LibraryStore`; missing or unknown values use liquid. `LocalGlassSkin` supplies the same value to routed screens and dialogs. | Either entry updates the same selection immediately and retains it after restart. No separate reader-only switch, shader controls, new route or reading-position key. |
| GLASS-SKIN-02 | Keep Haze 1.6.0 capture and combine it with Backdrop 2.0.1's actual rounded-rectangle refraction and directional-highlight shader source. Frosted controls use uniform diffusion; liquid controls use a clearer rim and a separately stabilized center. Text and icons remain foreground siblings outside the effect. | Background art is sampled from the real existing source, not redrawn or fabricated. The two choices have distinct materials while labels and page numbers remain readable. No solid black default panel or heavy full-screen gradient. |
| GLASS-SKIN-03 | Apply the shared material to reader chrome, settings/directory/spread dialogs, chapter-end previews/action, expanding search/results and existing artwork controls/info. Long-text panels use a stronger center wash. The progress and dim controls retain the 2dp track and Material Slider behavior; liquid uses a small 30×20dp glass thumb inside the same 36×24dp slot as frosted, with at least 48dp interaction height. | Changing skin does not replace chapter/page identities, reset zoom/scroll, seek, enqueue downloads or change comment state. Dragging still previews locally and commits once on release. Compact directory density and fixed toolbar bounds remain. |
| GLASS-SKIN-04 | Limit optical press feedback to current interactive surfaces and reuse shared motion timing. `trackPointer` defaults to false; passive floating comments remain outside hit testing. API 33+ and an enabled hardware blur path are required for the lens; unavailable shaders or older devices use the frosted/theme fallback without rewriting the selection. | Comment previews do not intercept reading gestures. Reduced motion disables decorative optical response. Unsupported drawing paths keep readable controls, with no animated render loop or new performance setting. |

Source adaptation: Backdrop **2.0.1**, commit `65ab177e90e5c1d8c62e70cf7755841982da65f6`, `backdrop/.../internal/Shaders.kt`, `effects/Lens.kt` and `highlight/HighlightStyle.kt`. The copied formulas live in `ui/glass/BackdropShaders.kt`; `BackdropGlassOptics.kt` binds them to Android RuntimeShader and adds a post-refraction readability wash. The original Apache-2.0 license and exact adaptation notice are bundled. Upstream: `https://github.com/Kyant0/AndroidLiquidGlass/tree/65ab177e90e5c1d8c62e70cf7755841982da65f6`.

This combines mature optics with the existing capture path; it does not import the complete Backdrop framework or claim Apple's full material, merging or morphing behavior. Downloaded dependency metadata showed that current Haze Glass 2.1.0 / Backdrop 2.0.1 AARs require compileSdk 37 and Kotlin 2.4 metadata, while Compose 1.12.1 requires AGP 9.1.0. Adapting the pinned optical source avoids an unrelated migration of this app's Kotlin 2.1.20 / AGP 8.7.3 / SDK 35 toolchain. Reconsider a complete library upgrade with a separately scoped toolchain change.

Evidence boundary: original sources, metadata and current diffs were read. Haze 1.6 supports ordinary Compose Dialog coordinates; its API 32+ cross-window pre-draw notification path does not establish continuous background refresh, so that behavior remains unverified. No tests, lint, local build, emulator or UI automation were run. Source review, direct-main submission and the CI trigger are delivery evidence; compilation, device appearance, performance and touch feel remain pending CI/user feedback. These four requirements describe the adopted implementation target, not a visual acceptance claim.

| ID | Decision and concrete implementation | Observable requirement |

| DETAIL-01 | A bounded Column contains navigation, a compact horizontal cover/info header, native metric badges, section tabs, the active content region and the reading action. No outer LazyColumn or overlay bar. | Chapters are the default visible content. No need to pass a cover, synopsis or comments to select a chapter. |
| DETAIL-02 | Adapt Mihon's fixed MangaAndSourceTitlesSmall composition: an 84×112dp cover, 16dp gutter and adjacent title/author/status/tags. Full title/metadata remain available in Introduction. Small-height/landscape/large-font layouts use a compact title header. | Artwork remains identifiable; the shared header leaves most available space for the task and can yield space to the keyboard. |
| DETAIL-03 | Adapt Ovio Liquid Tabs minimal into Compose: one indicator with separately animated leading/trailing edges, stationary labels and immediate content selection. Three sections: Chapters, Introduction, Comments. | Selected section is clear; rapid switching does not reset each section's saved position. No decorative counters or duplicate headings. |
| DETAIL-04 | Reflow existing chapter content into an adaptive grid. Compute rows from the bounded area and font scale; paginate the grid instead of extending the whole page. Adapt BoardUI's controlled pager and disabled boundaries, with 48dp actions and a range picker for many pages. | Ordinary portrait widths show roughly three columns; larger fonts reduce columns. Every chapter remains reachable; resizing preserves the anchor chapter where possible. |
| DETAIL-05 | A compact toolbar holds group selection, order, search, download mode and, when available, a last-read locator. Default to the first source group; All remains available. A visible download mode changes the tile action and shows its label. Long press additionally opens full chapter details/actions. | No repeated default-group subtitle in every tile. Read/download states remain distinguishable; downloading/queued/completed items cannot be enqueued again. |
| DETAIL-06 | Render the existing full comments panel directly in the Comments section. Keep provider selection above its bounded list, real avatars, replies, native metrics, matching, pagination and failures. Introduction has its own scroll area. | Switching sections preserves list positions. Comments need no second “all comments” sheet. Long content scrolls only within its own section. |
| DETAIL-07 | One compact reading action participates in normal layout and uses the existing onContinue path for saved progress. Catalog failures are an inline summary with detailed cause on demand. Wrap the detail route in the existing SaveableStateHolder. | A failed refresh cannot disable saved continuation. Returning from the reader restores the catalog section/group/search/page. The bottom action never covers chapters. |

## Motion system · 2026-11-12

Current request: “去深度优化我们现在漫画软件的动画系统可以深度学习下我们审美库先”. This slice changes motion and the state updates that disrupt it. Existing source/account contracts, artwork, dense layouts, reader modes and saved progress remain the product baseline. Other ongoing detail/home redesigns must be preserved when integrating.

| ID | Adopted mechanism and implementation | Observable requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| MOTION-01 | Shared press, swap, panel and navigation roles. Adapt beUI `a866cb2` physics/lifecycle and Ovio `81a1bcb` indicator edges; retain the approved Jelly reference's stationary labels and Josh reference's immediate press/release. Compose supplies the animation engine. | Repeated input retargets the current motion. No timers delay real actions or simulate completion. |
| MOTION-02 | Ovio leading/trailing springs animate only a tab's indicator. Press transforms and drawer depth read animated values in drawing layers. | Layout positions, touch targets and chapter dimensions stay stable; tab labels do not deform; no whole-list entrance on refresh. |
| MOTION-03 | Forward/back route transitions use opposite short translations, fixed viewport bounds and outgoing interaction gates. Vaul's actual drawer-offset coupling provides a small background recession only for root navigation. | Returning restores the existing saveable scroll/section state. Drawer dragging stays attached to the finger. Reader images never shrink with small tool dialogs. |
| MOTION-04 | Adapt beUI PresenceGate/MorphingModal: keep the outgoing visual until it settles, release its interaction/focus immediately, and animate only the panel. Bottom sheets hide before disposal. | Close/reopen can interrupt an exit; no invisible control receives actions, no stacked content makes a panel grow and shrink. |
| MOTION-05 | Reader chrome is an overlay with visibility-independent system insets. Scrubbing previews a page and commits once on release; horizontal history uses settled pages. Brightness previews locally before one persistence update. | Toolbars do not push artwork; dragging a slider does not decode or save every intermediate page. Preserve exact page/long-image position. |
| MOTION-06 | Short cache work gets delayed busy feedback, with fixed reserved slots. Known image aspect ratios survive thumbnail decoding. Icon changes use a fixed slot. | No flash of a spinner for a brief hit, no fake progress, no layout movement when download state changes. |
| MOTION-07 | Observe Android's animator-duration setting for reduced motion; disable additional spatial effects and carousel autoplay when disabled. Native gesture/animation timing still uses the platform clock. | All controls and status information remain usable with animations disabled. No completion depends on a decorative animation. |

### Reference evidence and adaptation

- beUI `a866cb2f37ce68c3fc0c66425e86e3f31566e4be`: `lib/ease.ts`, `lib/presence-gate.tsx`, `components/motion/morphing-modal.tsx`, MIT. Original code read; saved official modal/tab screenshots inspected. The screenshot deployment was not proven to match the archived commit. Adapt physics and presence ownership, without copying its full-screen blur or changing the reader viewport.
- Ovio `81a1bcb6737356038163728b3b2a657c9dcffa32`: existing Liquid Tabs reference above. Keep independent edges, stationary text and continuous retargeting. The existing catalog grouping/layout is outside this motion slice.
- Vaul `3e97aac6a38e4481bade71d7233ed6002e80f9b0` (1.1.2): `src/use-scale-background.ts`, `src/index.tsx`, MIT. Original video frame inspected. Adapt progress-coupled background depth to the existing side drawer; preserve Material's gesture arbitration instead of copying Web velocity units.
- Aesthetic-library records `uiux-motion-transition-system-20261110`, `uiux-motion-easing-kubai-20261106`, `uiux-doc-component-tabs-fluid`, `uiux-doc-component-button-tactile-review`: original motion-system text, saved approved tabs and Josh source/review read. These establish motion roles and prior preferences, not approval of this new Android implementation.
- Android official animation, graphicsLayer, DrawerState and bottom-sheet documentation was checked against the APIs used. Web stiffness/damping/mass are converted as `k/m` and `c/(2*sqrt(k*m))`; equal parameters do not guarantee identical frames across engines.

Implementation, main CI compilation, release packaging and phone experience are separate evidence. Follow AGENTS.md's current direct-main delivery rules: review source, submit to main and confirm CI triggered, without a separate branch build or waiting for completion. No tests, lint, emulator, UI automation or screenshot fixtures are permitted in this slice. Phone smoothness, visual quality and accessibility interaction remain pending user experience after delivery.

## Revised detail contract · 2026-11-12 (supersedes DETAIL-02 through DETAIL-05)

The user rejected the three-tab/paginated screenshot and explicitly requested the following:

- The reading action names the actual chapter; continuation keeps its saved page.
- The chapter grid uses small fixed-height tiles with an adaptive four-column tendency on portrait phones. The whole fetched directory is presented by a lazy grid and scrolls continuously, with no range picker or bottom pagination.
- The latest search-overlay follow-up removes the former chapter-tool drawer. Keep simple horizontal group switching and the download action in one fixed-height toolbar; remove chapter search, order and last-read locator controls.
- Download-mode selection must never insert a status row or resize a tile. The icon changes from download to progress, check or failure in a reserved slot without moving surrounding chapters.
- Real status/genre/update tags and provider metrics use multiple quiet, low-saturation accents. No fabricated tags, counts, ratings or providers.
- Keep exact chapter progress, anonymous source use, existing downloads, comments, and cache intact. The grid and comment list remain in a fixed content area beneath the header. No outer detail scroll, test suite or emulator automation for this change.

This section overrides older paging and section-transition advice in the same document. A successful CI build confirms compilation only; device interaction remains unverified.

Motion integration preserves this revised layout: the shared Ovio edge animation uses the actual compact text widths, 38dp tab height and 32dp underline; it does not restore the former capsule. The later motion feedback below adds a short content transition only when the user changes sections. Catalog icons keep their existing 14dp slots, and the fixed toolbar retains the latest mode label and icons. The user's “要啊直接合到 main 啊” explicitly authorizes this integration and the existing signed-release workflow; the current direct-main rules in AGENTS.md supersede the former branch-compilation gate.

### Reference and reuse evidence

- The requested `https://kayokoi.site/` returned public HTML declaring Sakurairo 3.0.10, repository mirai-mamori/Sakurairo, tag commit bd4a4a33f27da4a3b52baf9dccf0507cd5393f01. Live HTML has centerbg height 100vh and site_bg_as_cover=true. Same-version source `style.css` body uses background-attachment:fixed; `src/app/coverBackground.ts` assigns the body background; `src/app/global-func.js` headertop_down smoothly scrolls to content. Live body uses background-size:auto, so our portrait Crop is an explicit mobile adaptation, not a claim about the site.
- Same-version mobile navigation hides on downward scroll and reappears upward; this app keeps a small reachable navigation row for back/favorite and changes the content title with the header state. The fixed-artwork/foreground relationship and smooth content shortcut are adopted; blog waves, social controls, random artwork and desktop wheel interception are not part of this manga task.
- The site's live style/scripts failed to load in this environment (upstream 503 / smoothscroll 404), and the browser displayed its loader. Actual rendered site motion is unverified. Source evidence does not establish an unmodified live deployment. No scroll-driven zoom or blur was found; the theme's 0.8-second blur is an entry animation only.
- Sakurairo is GPL v2 or later. Its behavior is used as a reference; no theme code or assets are copied. The implementation reuses existing MangaFlow artwork, metadata and catalog components plus Apache-2.0 AndroidX APIs: Google Maven BOM 2025.05.01 maps to Material3 1.3.2 and Foundation/UI 1.8.2. Their published source JARs were read, including AppBar.kt ExitUntilCollapsed and Scrollable.kt pre/self/post dispatch.
- Source review is required; tests and UI automation remain prohibited. Per the latest explicit instruction, verify CI triggered and stop waiting. Compilation, actual device layout, image crop and gesture feel remain unverified until build/user feedback; do not call the new design visually accepted.

The shared-motion integration retains this large-artwork contract, its nested-scroll and shortcut cancellation behavior, and all Material theme roles. It only replaces the existing tab indicator, button feedback, icon swaps and sheet presence with the shared motion components. The original landscape Fit/Crop condition is cached at the outer layout scope to fix its Kotlin receiver error; artwork size and scrolling are unchanged by that repair.

## Floating search and liquid surfaces · 2026-11-12

Latest feedback: “点击后图标会有一些动画然后搜索框从图标左侧展出”. The input now reveals leftward from the fixed search icon within the existing 52dp toolbar, replacing the former large input panel below it. Keep the drawer visible; the search surface temporarily covers tabs/history and gates their pointer, focus and accessibility access. Use the shared press/transition roles for the small icon response and horizontal reveal. Text and hit targets do not stretch, closing reverses the same path, rapid toggles retarget the current animation, and reduced motion reaches the same usable states immediately.

Only a nonblank query opens the compact results overlay below the toolbar; an empty query has no large card. Query changes never replace, resize or scroll the retained browse content, and only the results overlay receives keyboard insets. Preserve the ViewModel background across configuration changes and separate per-query list positions. Retain input/result content until exit completes, while releasing focus and keyboard ownership immediately on dismissal or leaving the active route. Back, the surrounding scrim and the toolbar close action dismiss search; blank result-panel space must not trigger the scrim. Preserve the full-width carousel from `35217b8` and the shared motion from `112a2fe`.

`LiquidGlassSurface` shares the existing Haze 1.6.0 source/effect pair across search, artwork information and floating artwork controls. It samples the actual background, applies 12dp blur and theme tint, and adds a quiet highlight and rounded edge. Android 13+ refracts only the rim through an original AGSL shader. The shader is compiled once per surface, with dimensions passed as uniforms. Text and icons remain outside that optical layer. Android 12 retains blur; older devices retain a readable tinted surface. Glass on toolbar controls fades with header collapse, while the ordinary toolbar surface appears.

API references checked against the pinned dependency: Haze 1.6.0 usage（`https://github.com/chrisbanes/haze/blob/1.6.0/docs/usage.md`）, effect scope（`https://github.com/chrisbanes/haze/blob/1.6.0/haze/src/commonMain/kotlin/dev/chrisbanes/haze/HazeChild.kt`） and Android AGSL（`https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/views/graphics/agsl/using-agsl`）. No dependency upgrade, API request or storage migration is part of this change. Source review and the main CI trigger do not establish visual acceptance or device smoothness.

## Reader controls and continuous downloads · 2026-11-12

The latest reader screenshots reject the large black gradients, dark oversized dialogs, thick slider handles and sparse chapter rows. User correction, 2026-11-12: “章节的混合评论流不要混入作品的啊只保留章节的以及纵向和条漫其实是一个东西吧以及那个边看边下为什么要弄成开关？这种功能肯定是默认提供啊别暴露开关”.

Later download correction, 2026-11-12: “哦我理解错了是边下边看这个功能得有，边看边下这个功能删掉”. This replaces only the automatic-download interpretation; chapter-comment scope and unified vertical reading remain as specified.

- READER-SCOPE-01: the chapter feed requests and displays only comments belonging to the current chapter. Mixing means mixing genuine chapter sources, never substituting work comments. Copy uses exact chapter UUIDs; the source expansion adds Dantotsu only for confirmed AniList manga and unambiguous integer main-story chapters. All work comments stay in manga detail. Empty chapters show the ordinary empty state. Keep the existing source badges, avatars, dense rows, sorting, pagination, refresh and shared state owner.
- READER-MODE-01: both settings surfaces offer one vertical option alongside the two horizontal directions. Vertical reading uses the existing continuous, natural-height layout for ordinary pages and strips; ordinary pages retain zoom and long strips use the outer scroll surface. Stored WEBTOON maps to vertical without gaps. Old vertical long-image pan fractions convert into the outer offset after the real image height is known, and normal progress saving consumes that legacy value in the same write batch. Former WEBTOON offsets are already outer positions and must not receive an old inner-pan offset. Historical per-chapter mode switches were not recorded, so arbitrary mixed-mode legacy history cannot be proven exact. The full-height rendering path is inherited from WEBTOON; source review does not establish long-image memory use or gesture performance.
- READER-DOWNLOAD-01: downloading starts from an explicit download action. A pending or partial download can open directly without waiting for the whole chapter: use completed local images first and share an active transfer for missing images. Opening or changing chapters never creates a persistent download or queues background saving; ordinary reading retains its normal image cache. Remove the automatic-enqueue API and its preference machinery, with no new switch. Preserve individual pause, retry and delete intent, local-image priority and the full ordinary directory.

- READER-GLASS-01: follow-up “弄成液态玻璃会更好吧”, with screenshot `1000121363.jpg`, replaces the nearly opaque top and bottom reader plates with the existing `LiquidGlassSurface`. One stable Haze 1.6.0 source wraps the actual reader content and its dim layer; both toolbars sample it as separate foreground siblings. Retain the shared 12dp backdrop blur, theme tint, restrained edge highlight and Android 13+ rim refraction. Chapter labels and total page numbers use stronger foreground contrast over artwork. Keep existing dimensions, touch targets and show/hide motion, and never blur/refract the text or draw another copy of the comic. Independent-window dialogs keep their readable theme surface. The supplied screenshot is before-state evidence; source review and the main CI trigger do not establish device appearance or performance.
- Floating reader plates follow the app theme, with a subtle outline/shadow; paper color remains independent. The page and dim controls use a 2dp track, a small round thumb and at least a 48dp interaction area; engagement slightly expands only the drawn track/thumb. Current and total page numbers stay together in a reserved slot. Dragging previews locally and commits at release.
- Settings stay compact and scroll within short screens. The chapter dialog uses an adaptive grid, one group selector, a current-chapter selection and reserved saved/progress icons; it does not repeat the default group in each tile.
- A download row's cover/title opens its chapter and restores the saved image location. A small fixed action slot handles pause/resume/delete; there is no extra offline-reading CTA.
- The reader keeps original page identities and uses available local images page by page. An explicitly started download continues alongside reading and shares transfers with the worker. Only explicit user actions start downloads or restart paused or removed work. Full saved directories and online updates let local chapters continue into other chapters without automatically saving them.
- The reader's mixed comments label each source and contain only the current chapter. Chapter-end and toolbar entry points share the same state, cached rows and pagination; creating the state does not fetch until comments are requested. Copy and strictly mapped Dantotsu chapter comments share this path. Work-review requests, matching candidates and work-reply routes are absent from this reader flow. The direct comment sheet leaves image progress intact, and refresh retrieves current chapter content when the source is reachable.
- Compact comment rows keep the 28dp avatar, readable 13sp body and source/scope labels, with a short date on the same metadata line. Reduce row spacing and repeated headings rather than shrinking the body. Settings keep their header and close action available while the options scroll; the directory uses a dense adaptive grid and a quiet current-chapter state.
- The information card in the artwork header is a direct vertical scroll target. It and the empty artwork area share one state that consumes only the actual bounded header movement; the catalog and comment lists retain their original nested-scroll connection to that header. Child taps and horizontal tags retain Foundation's direction locking and cancellation. Do not attach both a direct header consumer and the appbar pre-scroll consumer to the same gesture path.
- The project homepage is the sole web navigation entry. Source pages, author pages and a separate releases link have no outward controls; documentation references remain inert code-formatted addresses with attribution and license text preserved.
- Source and diff review only. Build/release belongs to main CI/CD; compilation, device appearance, touch feel and live service behavior are not claimed as verified by this change.

The repeated blue primary favicon and a fixed 88%-height source sheet are observed; the nested CONTROL input samples and refracts detail comments. Ordinary one-version primary chapters return to one compact 48dp hit row with source ownership in the header/long press; alternate-source and multi-version chapters retain the 56dp badge region, with quiet monochrome real marks and X-STACK-01 overlap. The source manager wraps content under a 78%-viewport cap, uses existing Ovio Soft tabs and SearchScopeBar-style tonal input without a second backdrop sampler, automatically searches the work title on opening, and shows the chosen candidate with its confirmation instead of burying confirmation after all search results. Preserve explicit chapter-system confirmation, cancellation, actual results, priorities, detach and history. Source/diff review only; the revised phone layout remains pending user feedback, while the pictured panel material is user-liked rather than a newly verified rendering.

COPY-MARK-10 supersedes the Copy-favicon/standard MenuBook artwork only: “拷标我们要自己设计啊这个太恶心了吧你换了吗”. Add MangaFlow's own paired-page vector `ic_source_copy.xml`: two offset pages, one folded corner, restrained rounded strokes and two content lines, with neutral theme-aware tint and no backing shape/text. Use the same local resource in chapter/source-management/picker marks and all ReviewSourceMark callers (metrics, rating disclosure and comment filters). This is an application-authored source marker, not the site's official logo; no Copy favicon request or standard MenuBook substitute remains. Preserve UNIFIED-SOURCE-09's conditional chapter visibility, accepted panel frosting and X-STACK-01. Source review and asset inspection do not establish Android rendering acceptance.

## Automatic source coverage · 2026-11-13 (AUTO-SOURCE-11 / COPY-WORDMARK-11)

User: “你现在的源接入是不是有点少了连咒术回战这种漫画其他源都没有逗我呢多看看人家接了什么源以及社区讨论”. Later correction: “哦不对我弄错了反正你多加几个源以及当前这个 uiux 做的很不好很差劲以及拷贝的图标做的也很丑那就做个拷的图标不就行了吗以及是不是应该一进漫画就应该对所有源进行读取合并然后附图标才对啊现在的逻辑感觉不太对呢”. Final color decision: “拷字用低饱和蓝吧”. This supersedes manual-first UNIFIED-SOURCE-09 entry and rejects COPY-MARK-10's double-page artwork, while keeping the accepted panel frosting and X-STACK-01.

| ID | Current implementation and remaining evidence |
| --- | --- |
| AUTO-SOURCE-11A | Register Baozi, Manhuagui and MangaBZ alongside Copy/Komiic/MangaDex. Reuse pinned Keiyoushi Apache-2.0 source protocols/decoders; preserve original images and signed query strings. No remote JS execution, new JS runtime or image recompression. Source refusals/verification pages are failures, not zero results. |
| AUTO-SOURCE-11B | Remote work/detail and reader entry start independent background discovery; primary content stays readable. Share two request slots, give each source 25 seconds, reuse same-identity results for five minutes, retarget when full metadata arrives, and cancel older works' automatic scans. Never wait for every source before showing chapters. |
| AUTO-SOURCE-11C | Use full title/confirmed aliases, platform ICU simplified/traditional variants, verified detail authors (including iteration marks), and at least three ordinary matching serial chapter positions before automatic association. Preserve sequel ≡, novel, guide and edition distinctions. Multiple/uncertain candidates require correction in the overview; no ranking-based automatic merge. Atomic exclusions and manual choices prevent removed sources being re-added. |
| AUTO-SOURCE-11D | The source sheet opens to compact per-source results: checking, linked with actual chapter count, confirmation needed, no match, failure and disabled. Priority/detach stay inline; manual search and chapter-system confirmation are disclosed only when needed. No repeated search merely from opening the sheet. Keep the existing PANEL material/tint and content-fitting height; reserve stable 56dp unified chapter rows so arriving badges do not resize the matrix. |
| AUTO-SOURCE-11E | Komiic exposes only the two upstream-declared HTTPS origins, .com and .cc, with the chosen origin persisted in existing backup preferences. Requests/covers/images follow the selected origin; page-cache invalidation rejects stale manifest writes. Network failures remain distinct from no matches. |
| COPY-WORDMARK-11 | Replace the rejected vector with one shared “拷” typographic mark in SansSerif Medium. Use muted blue #567593 (light) / #A8BED2 (dark), adaptive size, no background, blue favicon or icon substitution. Apply it in source management, chapter marks, metrics, ratings and comment filters. |

Research actually read: [LinuxDO July 8–9 recommendations](https://linux.do/t/topic/2548395) list Komiic, Baozi, MangaBZ and the Keiyoushi/LittleSurvival ecosystems; [September 3 discussion](https://linux.do/t/topic/2847759) describes Baozi as incomplete/backup and recommends Manhuagui for the user's Chinese/Japanese titles. [Keiyoushi #16496](https://github.com/keiyoushi/extensions-source/issues/16496), opened June 6, records Komiic mainland access restrictions and .cc. Public indexed manga pages identify Jujutsu Kaisen on [Manhuagui](https://m.manhuagui.com/comic/28004/), [MangaBZ](https://www.mangabz.com/266bz/) and [Baozi](https://www.baozimh.com/comic/zhouzhuhuizhan-jiejianxia); these are catalogue/index evidence, not successful current app chapter/image requests. Venera's historical lists are not treated as current availability.

Source review and interface/diff inspection only under the current workflow; no local build, test, emulator or source API probing. Gatekeeper challenges and restricted content are not bypassed. Baozi's optional banner pixel rewriting was deliberately omitted to retain originals; image-response placeholder/redirect handling is still subject to the shared image transport. MangaBZ's complete long-volume manifests may reach the existing foreground deadline; verified batches are retained for retry, without exposing partial page lists. All three new source origins, merged results and revised phone interaction remain pending actual artifact/user feedback.
